# Third-party notices

Generated by `cargo xtask notices`. Do not edit this file by hand.

## Rust dependencies

Metadata comes from `cargo metadata`; `cargo tree` selects the normal dependency graph of `kanso-ffi` (Direct, MAS and Setapp, union of aarch64-apple-darwin and x86_64-apple-darwin), excluding workspace crates, dev dependencies and build-only tools. Proc-macro dependencies in that graph are included.

## License and notice texts

Files named LICENSE*, LICENCE*, COPYING* and NOTICE* are copied verbatim from each crate's manifest directory. Only exactly identical texts are grouped together.

### Crates without upstream license files

The following published crates contain no license file in their manifest directory. Standard texts for their declared SPDX licenses are included below, from https://github.com/spdx/license-list-data/tree/main/text. Template placeholders are retained; no copyright holder is inferred.

## Swift packages

The four packages under `apple/Packages` use local path dependencies only. There are no external Swift package dependencies or tracked Package.resolved files.

## Public test keys

The private keys under `crates/kanso-ssh/src/itests/data` are intentionally public test fixtures, used only by isolated tests. Never use them as real credentials.
